For coordinate p, affected lags m = p-b for pairs where a=p (p - b, b=0..n-1) and m = a-p where b=p (a=0..n-1). For each such m, contribution of p:
- if a=p: h_p(1-h_b), gradient wrt h_p: 1-h_b.
- if b=p: h_a(1-h_p), gradient: -h_a.
For m=0 diagonal: both a=p,b=p; gradient: (1-h_p) - h_p =1-2h_p; quadratic change as above.

Thus to accumulate gradient for weighted lags w_m, for all pairs (a,b) with weight w_{a-b}:
grad[a] += w_{a-b}*(1-h_b)
grad[b] -= w_{a-b}*(h_a?) Wait ∂/∂h_b of h_a(1-h_b) = -h_a. So grad[b] -= w * h_a.
This double counts diagonal: one add (1-h_p) and one subtract h_p total 1-2h_p. Good.

We can compute this via convolutions:

- i a=i+m, b=i, length n-m.
- if w[m]!=0:
  grad[m:] += w[m]*(1-h[:n-m])  (for a)
  grad[:n-m] -= w[m]*h[m:]      (for b) because h_a = h[m+m? wait a=i+m, b=i; subtract h_a from b. b index 0..n-m-1, h_a = h[i+m] = h[m:n]. So grad[:n-m] -= w[m]*h[m:]
For negative m=-r, r=1..n-1:
Pairs a=i, b=i+r, length n-r.
- grad[:n-r] += w[-r]* (1-h[r:n]) (a first)
- grad[r:] -= w[-r]* h[:n-r] (b second)

C5 eval:
```
def c5_value(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full'); return float(c.max())*dx
```

Gradient active:
```
def gradient_weighted(h, lags, weights):
    n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx,w in zip(lags, weights):
       if w==0: continue
       m=idx-(n-1)
       if m>=0:
          L=n-m
          # a=i+m, b=i
          g[m:] += w*(1.0-h[:L])
          g[:L] -= w*h[m:]
       else:
          r=-m; L=n-r
          # a=i, b=i+r
          g[:L] += w*(1.0-h[r:])
          g[r:] -= w*h[:L]
    return g
```
Check with diagonal m=0: g[:n] += w*(1-h); g[:n] -= w*h => (1-2h). Good.
Need weights maybe normalized to sum 1. For objective C = max c dx, gradient of soft active: if weights sum1, grad_c = ∑ w ∂c. Then multiply dx. We can return grad_c (counts) and scale step.

Use maintained c and evaluate only affected lags for pair, not full. Let's implement exact pair delta to be safe and efficient.

Pair (p,q), change h_p += d, h_q -= d (sum preserved). Let q change `-d`. Affected lags are those where p or q participates. We can collect unique lag indices `lags` (offsets m = idx-(n-1)) for p and q. For each lag, compute base c0, and new c(d) exactly.
How to compute new c for a given lag efficiently? We can derive delta as:
For each coordinate x with delta Δx (p: d, q: -d), contribution to c_lag = sum over pairs (a,b) with a-b=m of h_a(1-h_b). The delta for a lag can be computed by:
- For each changed coordinate x, if x is first (a=x): add Δx * (1 - h_b) for each b where lag m = x-b valid.
- if x is second (b=x): add -Δx * h_a for each a where m = a-x valid.
- If both changed and pair (x,y) with x first, y second? The above linear terms count the other coordinate using old values; we need second-order terms for lag where first and second are the two changed coords, and diagonal where x=y (only if same coordinate changed? For p with itself, Δx^2; for q with itself, Δq^2; for p,q distinct offdiag, cross term Δp*Δ(1-h_q) = -Δp*Δq? Let's derive using full local update for pairs involving changed coords:
For a lag, after changing p and q, c' = c + sum over affected pairs of change. We can compute by starting with old c, and for the small set of pairs where at least one coordinate changed, recompute their contribution difference using new h values. Instead of formulas, we can for each affected lag identify the pairs involving p or q and accumulate deltas exactly O(number of such pairs) per lag. But doing per lag heavy.

Alternative: For pair(d), we can compute new c for affected lags by using the linear formulas plus second-order corrections:
Let Δp=d, Δq=-d.
Unchanged pair products unchanged. Pairs involving p and/or q:
1. p as first with q as second (lag m=p-q): old h_p(1-h_q). New (h_p+d)(1-h_q+d) = old + d(1-h_q) + d*h_p? Wait 1-h_q' =1-h_q+d, h_p'=h_p+d. Change = d(1-h_q)+d h_p + d^2. Linear terms from p first: d(1-h_q), q second: -Δq? q second delta = -Δq * h_p = -(-d)h_p = d h_p. Plus cross: Δp*Δ(1-h_q)= d*d = d^2. Our linear terms sum d(1-h_q-h? wait q second subtract Δq*h_p? For coordinate q as second, pair p,q contribution coordinate q delta: derivative with respect h_q old is -h_p; delta_q=-d => change term = (-d)*(-h_p)=d h_p. Linear sum = d(1-h_q)+d h_p = d(1-h_q+h_p). Missing d^2. Actually old q factor (1-h_q); change due q is -Δq? If h_q changes by -d, factor changes by +d; h_p changes by d; cross d*d. So need second-order when both in same pair: Δp * Δ(1-h_q) = d*d.
2. q as first with p as second (lag m=q-p): old h_q(1-h_p). Change: h_q' =h_q-d, 1-h_p'=1-h_p-d. = old -d(1-h_p) -d h_q + d^2. Linear: q first -d(1-h_p), p second: Δp*(-h_q)= -d h_q. Cross (-d)*(-d)=+d^2.
3. p diagonal lag0: change = d(1-2h_p) - d^2 (since (h_p+d)-(h_p+d)^2 - old = d -2h_p d - d^2? yes -d^2).
4. q diagonal: Δ=-d => change = -d(1-2h_q) - d^2.
5. p as first with unchanged b: change d(1-h_b), no second-order (q unchanged in factor).
q as first with unchanged b: (-d)(1-h_b).
p as second with unchanged a: d? p second delta Δp*d? derivative -h_a, Δp=d -> -d h_a.
q as second with unchanged a: (-d)(-h_a)=d h_a.
6. If p and q same? not.
So delta_lag(d) = L_lag * d + Q_lag * d^2, where Q_lag is:
- -1 for diagonal p if lag=0 (index n-1) and p included? one diagonal contributes -1.
- -1 for diagonal q if same lag (0) -> if both, Q includes -2 at lag0.
- +1 for offdiag p first q second at lag p-q.
- +1 for offdiag q first p second at lag q-p.
If p-q = 0 no; if p=q no.
All other lags Q=0.
So we can precompute for pair:
- Linear coefficients L_lag: sum of contributions from p/q as first/second with old other values.
- Q_lag: as above (maybe 0,1,2).
Then c_new = c + L*d + Q*d^2 for each affected lag.
We can accumulate L and Q by looping over the affected lags once, without iterating pairs individually? We can derive L for p: for all lags where p first or second, add d*(1-h_b) or -d*h_a. - lag index for coordinate i diagonal = n-1: Q[n-1] += -1 for p, -1 for q (if same, -2).

def pair_cd(h,n,deadline,rng,n_trial=9):
  h=np.array(h,float); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmax=c.max()
  best=h,best_c=c5_value(h)
  slacks=[]
  # choose slack indices: all indices maybe; for pass in range:
  for pass in range(max_passes):
    if time>deadline break
    # pick interior slacks sorted by min(h,1-h) top S
    interior = np.argsort(np.minimum(h,1-h))[::-1]
    S=8 # or 16
    for s in interior[:S]:
       if h[s] <=1e-12 or h[s]>=1-1e-12: continue
       for p in range(n):
         if p==s: continue
         # only if p interior? If h_p=0 or1 maybe can still move to interior; interval may include.
         d = optimize_pair_update(h,c,p,s,n,n_trial,cmax)
         if d is not None:
            # values improved; update c
            # recompute cmax
            # update best if needed
    # periodic project/recompute
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cn=c5_value(h); if cn<best: best=h,best_c=cn
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    cmax=c.max()
    if no improvement in pass: break
```

Now low-dim Fourier start. If parent available, perhaps enough. But let's implement `fourier_start(n, rng, ntrials, deadline)`:
```
def fourier_start(n, rng, ntrials, deadline):
   x = 2*np.arange(n)/n
   M=6
   basis=[]
   for m in range(1,M+1):
      basis.append(np.cos(m*np.pi*x)); basis.append(np.sin(m*np.pi*x))
   basis=np.vstack(basis)
   best=None; bestc=1
   for i in range(ntrials):
     if time>deadline: break
     r=0.5+0.5*rng.rand()
     coeff=rng.normal(size=basis.shape[0])
     norm=np.linalg.norm(coeff)
     if norm<1e-12: continue
     g=r*(coeff/norm) @ basis
     h=0.5+0.5*g
     # Since r<=1 and coefficients unit max maybe g max <= sqrt(2M)? Actually sum of normalized coefficients dot basis can exceed1. Clip.
     h=np.clip(h,0,1)
     h=project_feasible(h,n)
     c=c5_value(h)
     if c<bestc: best=h,bestc=c
   return best,bestc
```
The normalized coefficient vector times basis: since each component of basis ∈[-1,1], g can exceed 1 by up to sqrt(dim)*r if coeff on sphere and basis not orthogonal? Actually if coeff norm 1, dot with row basis could exceed1 by sqrt(sum basis^2) up to sqrt(n)? But row vector length in n? The function pointwise sum coeff_m * trig; if coeff vector unit Euclidean over M, value amplitude ≤sqrt(∑ coeff² ∑? By Cauchy at fixed x, |g| ≤ ||coeff||_2 * sqrt(M) because there are 2M trig terms each ≤1, so can be sqrt(10)*1~3.16. Clipping projects. That's okay; we may explore saturated shapes.
Could instead construct a random signal with amplitude bounded: sample zeros? But okay.

Then h in [0,1] no clipping (except sum). This is better. For each trial, g = coeff@basis; g /= max(1, maxabs); h=0.5+0.5*g; project. Good. This preserves shape and max≤1. Sum projection minor.

def evaluate_pair_move(h, c, mask, L, Q, p, q, n, current_count):
    hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
    lo=max(-hp, hq-1.0); hi=min(1.0-hp, hq)
    if hi-lo < 1e-14: return None
    aff_idx = mask # boolean
    unaff = c[~aff_idx].max() if not aff_idx.all() else -inf
    c_aff = c[aff_idx]
    L_aff = L[aff_idx]
    Q_aff = Q[aff_idx]
    cands = make_cands(lo,hi,n_trial)
    best_d=0; best_val=current_count
    for d in cands:
       vals = c_aff + L_aff*d + Q_aff*d*d
       val = max(unaff, vals.max())
       if val < best_val:
          best_val=val; best_d=d
    return best_d,best_val
```

Implement `soft_grad` with conv full:
```
def soft_grad(x,n,beta,lam):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    m=c.max(); z=beta*(c-m); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); sw=w.sum()
    if sw==0: idx=nonzero(m); w[idx[i]]=1
    else w/=sw
    s=n-1
    first=np.convolve(w, 1.0-x, mode='full')
    grad=first[s:s+n].copy()
    wrev=w[::-1]
    second=np.convolve(wrev, x, mode='full')
    grad -= second[s:s+n]
    grad *= (2.0/n)
    if lam: grad += lam*(x.sum()-n/2.0)
    return grad
```
Need `first` length len(w)+n-1=3n-3; slice s to s+n valid? For first, w length2n-1, q length n, full length3n-3, desired indices s..s+n-1 (2s? max s+n-1 =2n-2 <3n-3). Good. For second same.
This computes weighted sum of c gradients exactly if weights correspond to lags. Nice.

Let's detail `project_feasible` with bisection:
```
def project_feasible(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0
    # if all clip? solve lambda
    lo=-float(h.min()) - 1e-12
    hi=1.0 - float(h.max()) + 1e-12
    if np.clip(h+lo,0,1).sum() > target: ... but monotonic increasing with lambda
    for _ in range(60):
        mid=(lo+hi)/2
        s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
        if s < target: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    hp=np.clip(h+lam,0,1)
    # fix residual
    diff=target-hp.sum()
    if diff>0:
        room=1.0-hp
        idxs=np.flatnonzero(room>1e-12)
        if idxs.size:
           # distribute proportionally
           r=room[idxs]; hp[idxs]+=diff*(r/r.sum())
        else: idx=...; hp[idx]+=diff
    elif diff<0:
        mass=hp.copy(); idxs=np.flatnonzero(mass>1e-12); if...
    return np.clip(hp,0,1)
```
But after distributing diff proportionally, may exceed 1 if large diff. Use iterative? diff from bisection should be tiny (≤1e-12) so okay. If initial flawed, maybe. We can do a loop:

  if diff>0: room=1-hp; tot=room.sum(); if tot<=1e-12 break

`lowpass_h`:
```
def lowpass_h(h, cutoff):
   y=np.fft.rfft(h)
   if cutoff >= len(y): return h
   yc=y.copy(); yc[cutoff+1:]=0
   hf=np.fft.irfft(yc, n=len(h))
   return project_feasible(hf)
```
Need `np.fft`. Good.

`fourier_candidate`: Preallocate? For multiple n calls, okay.
```
def fourier_candidate(n, rng, ntrials, deadline):
   x=2.0*np.arange(n)/n
   M=5
   cols=[]
   for m in range(1,M+1):
      cols.append(np.cos(m*np.pi*x)); cols.append(np.sin(m*np.pi*x))
   B=np.vstack(cols)
   best=None; bestc=1e9
   for i in range(ntrials):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      coeff=rng.normal(size=B.shape[0])
      norm=np.linalg.norm(coeff)
      if norm<1e-12: continue
      g=(coeff/norm) @ B
      amax=np.max(np.abs(g))
      if amax>1.0: g/=amax
      h=0.5+0.5*g
      h=project_feasible(h)
      c=c5_value(h)
      if c<bestc:
         bestc=c; best=h.copy()
   return best,bestc
```
Could also include deterministic first harmonic? Parent likely better.

`lbfgs_polish`:
```
def lbfgs_polish(h, n, deadline):
   best_h=h.copy(); bestc=c5_value(h)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   betas=[20.0,100.0,400.0]
   lams=[1e3]
   for beta in betas:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      remaining=deadline-time.time()
      maxiter=max(50, min(250, int(remaining/0.3)))? Can't rely. Use 150.
      for lam in lams:
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         try:
            res=minimize(soft_obj, best_h, args=(n,beta,lam), jac=soft_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-18, 'gtol':1e-12})
            hn=project_feasible(res.x)
            cn=c5_value(hn)
            if cn<bestc:
               best_h=hn; bestc=cn
         except Exception: pass
   return best_h,bestc
```

Now, multi-candidate flow in run:
```

  global _DL
  t0=time.time(); overall_deadline=t0+budget_s-8
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  # get parent
  try: h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
  except: n0=500; h0=fourier_candidate(... maybe)
  h0=project_feasible(h0); c0=c5_value(h0)
  best_h=h0; best_c=c0
  # generate alternatives, choose maybe parent and fourier
  # low-pass of parent maybe
  candidates=[]

  # lowpass cutoffs:

```
def pair_cd(h, n, deadline, rng, n_slack=6, max_random=20000):
    h = np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')
    cmax = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = cmax
    dx=2.0/n
    # but cmax counts
    for pass_no in range(1000):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        improved_pass=0
        # choose slacks
        interior = np.argsort(np.minimum(h, 1.0-h))[::-1]
        slacks=interior[:n_slack]
        for s in slacks:
            if h[s] <= 1e-12 or h[s] >=1-1e-12: continue
            for p in range(n):
                if p==s: continue
                res=pair_move(h,c,p,s,n,cmax)
                if res is not None:
                   d,val=res
                   h[p]+=d; h[s]-=d
                   # clip tiny
                   if h[p]<0 or h[p]>1 or h[s]<0 or h[s]>1: ... recompute?
                   # update c
                   # pair_move could return mask,L,Q? To avoid recompute. We can have pair_move return d, val, mask,L,Q.
```
We need mask/L/Q for update. Have `try_pair` return `(d, val, mask, L, Q)` if improvement. But returning large arrays per update heavy. Instead `pair_step` can directly update h,c and return val. It can allocate arrays and if best, use them to update. So:
```
def pair_step(h,c,p,q,n,current_max, rng=None):
   ... if best_d !=0 and best_val < current_max:
       h[p]+=best_d; h[q]-=best_d

       return float(best_val)

```

Inside `pair_step`, build mask, L,T? Need Q full. Implementation:
```
def pair_step(h,c,p,q,n,current_max):
    hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
    lo=max(-hp, hq-1.0); hi=min(1.0-hp, hq)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-15: return None
    L=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    Q=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    mask=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=bool)
    rev_h = h[::-1]
    # p delta +1
    mask[p:p+n]=True
    mask[n-1-p:2*n-2-p+1]=True
    L[p:p+n] += (1.0 - rev_h)
    L[n-1-p:2*n-2-p+1] -= h
    # q delta -1
    first_delta=-1.0
    L[q:q+n] += first_delta*(1.0 - rev_h)
    L[n-1-q:2*n-2-q+1] -= first_delta*h  # since -delta*h = -(-1)*h=+h
    # Equivalent to L -= (1-rev); L += h.
    mask[q:q+n]=True; mask[n-1-q:2*n-2-q+1]=True
    diag=n-1
    Q[diag] -= 2.0
    idx1=diag + (p-q)
    if 0 <= idx1 < len(L): Q[idx1] += 1.0
    idx2=diag + (q-p)
    if 0 <= idx2 < len(L): Q[idx2] += 1.0
    c_aff = c[mask]
    L_aff = L[mask]
    Q_aff = Q[mask]
    # unaffected max
    if np.all(mask):
       unaff = -1.0
    else:
       unaff = float(c[~mask].max())
    cands=make_cand_d(lo,hi)
    best_d=0.0; best_val=current_max
    for d in cands:
        vals = c_aff + L_aff*d + Q_aff*(d*d)
        v = vals.max()
        if v < unaff: v=unaff
        if v < best_val:
             best_val=v; best_d=d
    if best_d !=0.0 and best_val < current_max - 1e-12:
       h[p]+=best_d; h[q]-=best_d
       # clip round maybe ensure exact bounds by tiny
       if h[p] < 0: h[p]=0.0; ... but sum? rare
       c[mask] = c_aff + L_aff*best_d + Q_aff*(best_d*best_d)
       return float(best_val)
    return None
```
Need add q contributions correctly. Let's check signs:
For p delta +1: first `L[p:p+n] += (1-rev_h)`, second `L[n-1-p:...] -= h`.
For q delta -1: first contribution Δ=-1: `L[q:q+n] += -1*(1-rev_h) = -(1-rev_h)`. second contribution Δ=-1: `L[n-1-q:...] -= (-1)*h = +h`. Good.
But we reuse rev_h = h[::-1] (old). Good.

Mask ranges: first x: x to x+n-1 inclusive length n: slice `x:x+n`. second x: start `n-1-x`, end `2*n-2-x` inclusive: slice `n-1-x : 2*n-2-x+1` = `n-1-x : 2*n-1-x`. Since 2n-2-x+1=2n-1-x. Use that. Need ensure within array length 2n-1. For x=0 second start n-1 end 2n-2 OK. For x=n-1 second start0 end n-2.
```
L[n-1-q : 2*n-1-q] += h  # length n
```
Good.

Q offdiag indices: m=p-q; index = n-1 + m. With s=n-1. `idx1 = n-1 + p - q`. valid always? p-q ∈[-n+1,n-1] => index 0..2n-2. Similarly idx2. Add +1 to both. Diagonal Q[diag] -=2. But consider if p and q offdiag index equals diag? That would p=q, excluded. Good.

Is Q coefficient for diagonal both always -2? The lag c_0 includes h_p(1-h_p) and h_q(1-h_q). change due p: d(1-2hp) - d^2. q: -d(1-2hq) - d^2. Total linear d(1-2hp +1-2hq?) Wait q: Δ=-d. Diagonal change formula for Δ: Δ(1-2h) - Δ². For q: -d(1-2hq)-d² = d(2hq-1)-d². Sum linear = d(1-2hp) + d(2hq-1) = d(2h_q -2h_p). Our L diagonal from p: (1-hp) from first and -hp from second? L[p] at diag =1-2hp. From q delta -1: first -(1-hq) + second +hq = 2hq-1. Sum =1-2hp+2hq-1 =2hq-2hp. Good. Q -2. Good.

def lp_active_step(h, n, deadline, n_active=30, solver=None):
    if cp is None: return h, c5_value(h)

def lp_polish(h,n,deadline, n_active=30):
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_value(h)

Now, one_lag_grad for LP:
```
def one_lag_grad(h, idx, n):
    g=np.zeros(n); m=idx-(n-1)
    if m>=0:
       L=n-m; g[m:] += 1-h[:L]; g[:L] -= h[m:]
    else:
       r=-m; L=n-r; g[:L] += 1-h[r:]; g[r:] -= h[:L]
    return g
```
This matches all-lag gradient with weight1.

Projection:
```
def project(h):
 h=np.asarray(h,float); n=len(h); target=.5*n
 lo=-h.min(); hi=1-h.max()
 for _ in range(50):
   mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
   if s<target: lo=mid
   else: hi=mid
 hp=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
 diff=target-hp.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-12:
   if diff>0:
     r=1-hp; idx=np.flatnonzero(r>1e-13)
     if idx.size: hp[idx]+=diff*r[idx]/r[idx].sum()
   else:
     idx=np.flatnonzero(hp>1e-13)
     if idx.size: hp[idx]+=diff*hp[idx]/hp[idx].sum()
 return np.clip(hp,0,1)
```
If r sum zero? not.

```
def sob(x,n,beta,lam):
 c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full'); m=c.max()

```

def lbfgs(h,n,dl):
 best=h.copy(); bc=eval_c(best)*2/n
 bounds=[(0.,1.)]*n
 _D=dl-3
 for beta in (5.,20.,100.):
   if time.time()>dl: break
   try:
     res=minimize(sob,best,args=(n,beta,100.),jac=sgrad,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,callback=cb,options={'maxiter':150,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-10})
     hn=project(res.x); cn=eval_c(hn)*2/n
     if cn<bc: best=hn; bc=cn
   except: pass
 return best,bc
```

  l=n-m; g[m:]+=1-h[:l]; g[:l]-=h[m:]

def cands(lo,hi):
 if hi<=lo+1e-14: return np.array([])
 v=[lo,hi]
 for m in (1e-6,1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.05,0.1,0.25):
   if -m>lo and -m<hi: v.append(-m)
   if m>lo and m<hi: v.append(m)
 span=hi-lo
 for f in (.1,.25,.5,.75,.9): v.append(lo+span*f)
 a=np.asarray(v,float)
 a=a[(a>lo+1e-14)&(a<hi-1e-14)]
 if a.size: a=np.unique(a)
 return a
```

`pair_step`: compact but careful. Use `cand_len = len(candarr)`.
```
def pair_step(h,c,p,q,n,cmx):
 hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
 lo=max(-hp,hq-1); hi=min(1-hp,hq)
 if hi<=lo+1e-14: return None
 L=np.zeros(2*n-1); Q=np.zeros(2*n-1); mask=np.zeros(2*n-1,bool)
 rh=h[::-1]
 L[p:p+n]+=1-rh; L[n-1-p:2*n-1-p]-=h; mask[p:p+n]=True; mask[n-1-p:2*n-1-p]=True
 L[q:q+n]-=1-rh; L[n-1-q:2*n-1-q]+=h; mask[q:q+n]=True; mask[n-1-q:2*n-1-q]=True
 Q[n-1]-=2; Q[n-1+p-q]+=1; Q[n-1+q-p]+=1
 if mask.all(): unaff=-np.inf
 else: unaff=float(c[~mask].max())
 ca=c[mask]; la=L[mask]; qa=Q[mask]
 arr=cands(lo,hi)
 bd=0; bv=cmx
 for d in arr:
   mm=(ca+la*d+qa*d*d).max()
   if unaff>mm: mm=unaff
   if mm<bv: bv=float(mm); bd=d
 if bd and bv<cmx-1e-12:
   h[p]+=bd; h[q]-=bd
   c[mask]=ca+la*bd+qa*bd*bd
   return float(bv)
 return None
```
Need index `Q[n-1+p-q]` valid: p-q range, sum 0..2n-2. Since n-1+p-q = n-1 + (p-q). Good. `Q[n-1+q-p]` too. Diag n-1.

Potential bug: For q second contribution slice, end `2*n-1-q`. If q=0, start n-1, end 2n-1 => slice length n (indices n-1..2n-2), ok. If q=n-1, start 0, end n => length n (0..n-1), but second range should 0..n-2 length n-1? Wait recalc second range for x: a=0..n-1, index = n-1+a-x. For x=n-1: indices 0..n-2 length n-1, not n? But there are n possible a? Let's check x=n-1 (last coordinate): as second, pairs (a, n-1) for a=0..n-1. For a=n-1 (diagonal) included index n-1+n-1-(n-1)=n-1. Wait x=n-1, a=n-1 -> index n-1. Range a 0..n-1: index n-1-(n-1)=0 to n-1. That's n values 0..n-1. My earlier thought 0..n-2 missed diagonal. For x=0: a=0..n-1 indices n-1..2n-2 length n. Good. So second slice for x=n-1: start n-1-x=0, end 2n-2-x+1=2n-2-(n-1)+1=n; slice 0:n includes 0..n-1. Good. For x=0: start n-1, end 2n-1, slice n-1:2n-1 includes n-1..2n-2 length n. Good. So `2*n-1-q` correct.
First range x: b=0..n-1, index n-1+x-b -> x to x+n-1? For x=n-1: x..x+n-1 = n-1..2n-2 length n. For x=0:0..n-1. Slice x:x+n correct.
Mask union length maybe 2n-1 all. Good.

`Q[n-1+p-q]`: for p=0,q=n-1 => n-1-(n-1)=0, valid. For p=n-1,q=0 => n-1+(n-1)=2n-2.

`L[p:p+n] += 1-rh`: For x as first, b=n-1, ...,0. `rh` = h[n-1],...,h[0]. Slice length n, correct.
`L[n-1-p:2*n-1-p] -= h`: For x as second, a=0..n-1, h. Length? end-start=(2n-1-p)-(n-1-p)=n, correct.
For q delta -1: first `L[q:q+n] += - (1-rh)`, second `L[n-1-q:2n-1-q] -= -1*h` => `+= h`. Good.

def pair_cd(h,n,dl,rng,slack=8):
 h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmx=c.max(); best=h.copy(); bestc=cmx; target=n/2

   passes+=1; imp=0
   order=np.argsort(np.minimum(h,1-h))[::-1]
   for s in order[:slack]:
     if s==? and h[s]<=1e-12 or >=: continue
     for p in range(n):
       if p==s: continue
       if time.time()>dl-1: break
       r=pair_step(h,c,p,s,n, cmx)
       if r is not None: cmx=r; imp+=1
     if time.time()>dl-1: break

`optimize_candidate` uses counts. Need implement:
```
def optimize_candidate(h,n,dl,rng):
   h=project(h); bc=eval_c(h)*2/n; best=h
   # lp polish
   lp_dl=min(dl-120, time.time()+120)
   if time.time()<lp_dl:
      _DL=lp_dl-2
      h1,bctl=lp_polish(h,n,lp_dl)
      c1=bctl*2/n
      if c1<bc: h,bc=project(h1),c1

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

_D=[0.0]

def _cb(x):
    return time.time() > _D[0]

def project(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    n=len(h); target=0.5*n
    if n==0: return h
    lo=-float(np.min(h)); hi=1.0-float(np.max(h))
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s<target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    hp=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)
    diff=target-hp.sum()
    if diff>0:
       room=1.0-hp; idx=np.flatnonzero(room>1e-14)
       if idx.size: hp[idx]+=diff*room[idx]/room[idx].sum()
    elif diff<0:
       idx=np.flatnonzero(hp>1e-14)
       if idx.size: hp[idx]+=diff*hp[idx]/hp[idx].sum()
    return np.clip(hp,0,1)
```
If room idx empty, diff remains; but not.

`eval_c` as above.

`sob` and `sgrad`: Use `beta` maybe 0? no.

```
def sob(x,n,beta,lam):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    m=float(c.max())
    z=beta*(c-m)
    e=np.exp(z)
    val=m+float(np.log(e.sum()))/beta
    err=float(x.sum()-0.5*n)
    return val*(2.0/n)+0.5*lam*err*err
```
If beta very large and c-m all zeros except max, e sum positive. Good.

`sgrad`: If lam==0 skip err. `g += lam*err` adds scalar to each element. If lam is int. Good.

LP:
```
def lag_grad(h,idx,n):
   g=np.zeros(n); m=idx-(n-1)
   if m>=0:
      l=n-m; g[m:]+=1.0-h[:l]; g[:l]-=h[m:]
   else:
      r=-m; l=n-r; g[:l]+=1.0-h[r:]; g[r:]-=h[:l]
   return g

def lp_1(h,n,na):
   if cp is None: return None
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   mx=float(c.max())
   if c.size>na:
      idx=np.argpartition(c,-na)[-na:]
   else:
      idx=np.arange(c.size)
   extra=np.flatnonzero(c > mx-0.75)
   if extra.size: idx=np.union1d(idx,extra)
   idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
   if idx.size>70: idx=idx[:70]
   t=cp.Variable(); H=cp.Variable((n,1))
   cons=[H>=0.0, H<=1.0, cp.sum(H)==0.5*n]
   hf=h.reshape(n,1)
   for ii in idx:
      g=lag_grad(h,int(ii),n).reshape(n,1)
      cons.append(t >= float(c[ii]) + cp.sum(g*(H-hf)))
   prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t),cons)
   status=None
   for sol in ('PDLP','ECOS','SCS'):
      try:
         prob.solve(solver=sol, verbose=False)
         status=prob.status
      except Exception:
         status='error'
      if status in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate'):
         break
   if status not in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate') or H.value is None: return None
   xn=project(np.asarray(H.value).reshape(n))
   bestc=eval_c(h); best=h
   for a in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.05,0.02):
       cand=project(h + a*(xn-h))
       cc=eval_c(cand)
       if cc<bestc:
          bestc=cc; best=cand
   if bestc < eval_c(h)-1e-12:
      return best,bestc
   return None
```
Calling eval_c(h) twice; store `basec`.
If solver not installed, loops error. Good.

`lp_polish`:
```
def lp_polish(h,n,dl):
    h=project(h); bc=eval_c(h); stall=0; na=30
    while time.time()<max(time.time()+1,dl-2) and stall<3:
        r=lp_1(h,n,na)
        if r is None: stall+=1
        else:
            nh,nc=r
            if nc<bc-1e-12:
                h=nh; bc=nc; stall=0
            else: stall+=1
        na=min(80,na+10)
    return h,bc
```
`max(time.time()+1,dl-2)` not necessary. Use `while time.time() < dl - 2 and stall<3`.

`lbfgs`:
```
def lbfgs(h,n,dl):
    best=project(h); bc=eval_c(best)*(2.0/n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    _D[0]=dl-2
    for beta in (5.0,20.0,80.0):
        if time.time()>dl-2: break
        try:
            res=minimize(sob,best,args=(n,beta,100.0),jac=sgrad,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,callback=_cb,options={'maxiter':150,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-10})
            hn=project(res.x)
            cn=eval_c(hn)*(2.0/n)
            if cn<bc:
                best=hn; bc=cn
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best,bc
```
If beta 80 and penalty, okay.

Maybe use lam sequence? If `sob` penalty 100 always. Good.

def lowpass(h,cut):
   if cp? no
   y=np.fft.rfft(h); yc=y.copy(); if cut<len(y): yc[cut+1:]=0; hf=np.fft.irfft(yc,n=len(h)); return project(hf)
   return project(h)
```

`fourier_start`:
```
def fourier_start(n,rng,nt,dl):
   x=2.0*np.arange(n)/n
   B=[]
   for m in range(1,6):
      B.append(np.cos(m*np.pi*x)); B.append(np.sin(m*np.pi*x))
   Bv=np.asarray(B)
   best=None; bc=1e9
   for i in range(nt):
      if time.time()>dl: break
      coeff=rng.normal(size=Bv.shape[0])
      nr=float(np.linalg.norm(coeff))
      if nr<1e-12: continue
      g=(coeff/nr)@Bv
      amax=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
      if amax>1.0: g/=amax
      h=0.5+0.5*g
      h=project(h)
      c=eval_c(h)*(2.0/n)
      if c<bc:
         bc=c; best=h
   return best,bc
```
If best none, returns None.

Pair functions as above. Let's write carefully.

`cands_d`:
```
def cands_d(lo,hi):
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    v=[lo,hi]
    mags=(1e-6,1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.05,0.1,0.25)
    for m in mags:
        if -m > lo and -m < hi: v.append(-m)
        if m > lo and m < hi: v.append(m)
    span=hi-lo
    for f in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9):
        d=lo+span*f
        if d>lo+1e-14 and d<hi-1e-14: v.append(d)
    a=np.asarray(v,float)
    a=a[(a>lo+1e-14)&(a<hi-1e-14)]
    if a.size:
        a=np.unique(a)
    return a
```
Endpoints included but then filtered out because require >lo and <hi; so endpoints not included. Should we include endpoints? Could be useful if best at bound. But if endpoint, h becomes exactly 0/1; due floating maybe okay. But if we exclude, small moves near endpoint from mag/quantiles cover. If interval very small, no candidates. Maybe include endpoints with a tiny inset? We can include endpoints if interval length >1e-12 but they may cause bound and future moves limited. Not necessary. Keep interior.

`pair_step` as above. Need `nonzero`? no.
```
def pair_step(h,c,p,q,n,cmx):
    hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
    lo=max(-hp,hq-1.0); hi=min(1.0-hp,hq)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14: return None
    size=2*n-1
    L=np.zeros(size); Q=np.zeros(size); mask=np.zeros(size,dtype=bool)
    rh=h[::-1]
    L[p:p+n] += 1.0-rh; L[n-1-p:2*n-1-p] -= h
    mask[p:p+n]=True; mask[n-1-p:2*n-1-p]=True
    L[q:q+n] -= 1.0-rh; L[n-1-q:2*n-1-q] += h
    mask[q:q+n]=True; mask[n-1-q:2*n-1-q]=True
    Q[n-1]-=2.0
    Q[n-1+p-q]+=1.0
    Q[n-1+q-p]+=1.0
    if mask.all(): unaff=-np.inf
    else: unaff=float(c[~mask].max())
    base=c[mask]; la=L[mask]; qa=Q[mask]
    arr=cands_d(lo,hi)
    bd=0.0; bv=float(cmx)
    for d in arr:
       mm=float(np.max(base + la*d + qa*d*d))
       if unaff > mm: mm=unaff
       if mm < bv:
          bv=mm; bd=d
    if bd!=0.0 and bv < cmx - 1e-12:
       h[p] += bd; h[q] -= bd
       c[mask] = base + la*bd + qa*bd*bd
       return float(bv)
    return None
```
Potential issue: `base=c[mask]` is view? It is copy? Boolean indexing returns copy. Then `c[mask] = base + ...` okay. If `bd` chosen, `base` old. Good. If update changes c values used in future.
If `hi`/`lo` endpoints not included, no exact bound moves; okay.
If arr includes very small d ~1e-6, vector op `la*d` may underflow? fine.

`pair_cd`:
```
def pair_cd(h,n,dl,rng):
    h=project(np.asarray(h,float)).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmx=float(c.max())
    best=project(h); bestc=eval_c(best)
    target=0.5*n
    slack=min(5,max(2,n//100))
    passes=0
    while time.time() < dl - 2:
        passes +=1
        imp=0
        order=np.argsort(np.minimum(h,1.0-h))[::-1]
        for s in order[:slack]:
           if h[s] <=1e-12 or h[s]>=1-1e-12: continue
           for p in range(n):
              if p==s: continue
              if time.time()>dl-2: break
              r=pair_step(h,c,int(p),int(s),n,cmx)
              if r is not None:
                 cmx=r; imp+=1
           if time.time()>dl-2: break
        if imp==0:
           no=0
           while time.time()<dl-2 and no<3000:
              p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
              if p==q: no+=1; continue
              r=pair_step(h,c,p,q,n,cmx)
              if r is not None:
                 cmx=r; no=0; imp=1
              else: no+=1
           if imp==0: break
        if passes % 5 == 0 or abs(h.sum()-target) > 1e-9:
           hp=project(h); pc=eval_c(hp)
           if pc <= cmx + 1e-9:
              h=hp; c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmx=float(c.max())
              if pc < bestc:
                 bestc=pc; best=h.copy()
           else:
              h=best.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmx=float(c.max())
              bestc=cmx
    hp=project(h); pc=eval_c(hp)
    if pc < bestc:
       bestc=pc; best=hp
    bp=project(best); bpc=eval_c(bp)
    if bpc < bestc:
       bestc=bpc; best=bp
    return best, bestc*(2.0/n)
```

def optimize_candidate(h,n,dl,rng):
   h=project(h); bc=eval_c(h)*(2.0/n); best=h
   # LP phase
   lp1 = min(dl - 180, time.time() + 120)
   if time.time() < lp1 - 5:
      h1, bc1 = lp_polish(h, n, lp1)
      if bc1 < bc: h,bc = project(h1), bc1
   # LBFGS phase
   lbdl = dl - 160
   if time.time() < lbdl - 5:
      h1, c1 = lbfgs(h, n, lbdl)
      if c1 < bc:
         h,bc=project(h1),c1
   # LP phase 2
   lp2 = min(dl - 60, time.time() + 80)
   if time.time() < lp2 - 5:
      h1,bc1=lp_polish(h,n,lp2)
      if bc1<bc: h,bc=project(h1),bc1
   # Pair
   if time.time() < dl - 3:
      h1,c1=pair_cd(h,n,dl-3,rng)
      if c1<bc: h,bc=project(h1),c1
   h=project(h); bc=eval_c(h)*(2.0/n)
   return h,bc
```

```
def cand_key(item): return item[1]

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); overall=t0+budget_s-6
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       raw=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n=int(len(raw))
       parent=project(raw); pc=eval_c(parent)*(2.0/n)
    except Exception:
       n=350
       parent,pc=fourier_start(n,rng,100,t0+30)
       if parent is None:
          parent=project(0.5+0.25*np.sin(np.pi*np.arange(n)*?))
          pc=eval_c(parent)*2/n
    best=parent; bestc=pc
    alts=[]
    # lowpass
    if n>=80 and time.time()<overall-150:
       for cut in (3,8):
          hl=lowpass(parent,cut); cl=eval_c(hl)*(2.0/n)
          if cl < bestc + 0.002:
             alts.append((hl,cl))
    # downsample
    if n>320 and time.time()<overall-200:
       ns=ns_even = 320? Use 320.
       x=2*np.arange(n)/n; xs=2*np.arange(ns)/ns
       hd=project(np.interp(xs,x,parent)); cd=eval_c(hd)*(2.0/ns)
       if cd < bestc + 0.002: alts.append((hd,cd))
    # fourier at n
    if time.time()<overall-250:
       hf,cf=fourier_start(n,rng,150,min(overall-200,time.time()+60))
       if hf is not None and cf < bestc + 0.002: alts.append((hf,cf))
    # also fourier at 320 if n large
    if time.time()<overall-300 and n>320:
       hf2,cf2=fourier_start(320,rng,100,overall-250)
       if hf2 is not None and cf2 < bestc+0.002: alts.append((hf2,cf2))
    alts.sort(key=cand_key)
    # parent optimize
    rem=overall-time.time()
    if rem>120:
       dlp=time.time()+min(rem-30, max(180, int(0.6*rem)))
       hp,cp=optimize_candidate(best,n,dlp,rng)
       if cp<bestc: best,bestc=hp,cp
    # alternatives
    for idx,(ha,ca) in enumerate(alts):
       rem=overall-time.time()
       if rem<90: break
       if ca > bestc + 0.002: continue
       if len(ha)==len(best): # skip duplicate parent? but alts only lowpass etc len same maybe. okay
          pass
       share=[0.45,0.3,0.25][idx] if idx<3 else 0.15
       dln=time.time()+max(60, share*rem-5)
       if dln>overall-15: dln=overall-15
       if dln-time.time()<30: break
       ho,co=optimize_candidate(ha,len(ha),dln,rng)
       if co<bestc: best,bestc=ho,co
    best=project(best)
    bestc=eval_c(best)*(2.0/len(best))
    return best,bestc,len(best)
```

Perfect.

Pair_step will find d maybe to reduce max. Good.

Now, one subtlety: In pair Step update c[mask] = base + la*d + qa*d*d. But base is c[mask] old. For diagonal and offdiag, correct. For lags affected by p and q where both contributions but not the same pair? Could there be a lag that includes p as first with some b and q as second with same a? We accounted offdiag if p and q are the two coordinates and lag p-q or q-p. If there is a lag where p as first with b=q (offdiag) and also q as first with a=p? Same lag? For distinct p,q, these are opposite lags p-q and q-p, different unless p=q. So handled. No other cross. Good.

Pair_step's Q q full allocated; if idx1/idx2 outside? p,q indices valid, but due int? `n-1+p-q` min 0 max 2n-2. Good.

If mask all, `unaff=-np.inf`, `mm` from vals. If vals empty? mask all, base nonempty. If n=1, p/q pair none. pair_cd random p==q so no. okay.

Now, code final. Ensure `fourier_start` uses `np.pi`, no import math.

Let's compose final answer.

But before final, consider if `cvxpy` import at top can be slow? okay. If not available, cp None. If solve uses SCS which maybe not installed but import cp exists, try loops. If a solver attempt raises `ValueError` due solver unavailable? catch. If it hangs? not.

Potential issue: `prob.solve(solver=sol, verbose=False)` for ECOS with LP with equality and inequality may produce `optimal_inaccurate`. H.value probably. Good.

Potential issue: In `lp_1`, if `prob.status` from previous solver remains optimal but H.value from failed? We only proceed if status in optimal and H.value None check. Good.

Time budget: The run may exceed if LP or LBFGS call long and no callback in LP. But LP small; okay. LBFGS callback. Pair loops time checked. Fouriер time checked.

If `budget_s` small (e.g., 100), allocations may skip. In run, `overall=t0+budget_s-6`. If budget_s small, parent optimize maybe rem>150? no skip; final returns initial. Good. But default 1100.

Potential issue: `np.interp(xs,x,parent)` for n=0? no.

Potential issue: If parent n is 500, lowpass h may C slightly lower; alts maybe. `optimize_candidate` on parent uses LP and LBFGS; if parent already optimized, LP line search might reject all and stall after 3 attempts. Good.

Could there be an even better simple shape we haven't tried? Our search should find.

Let's consider if `initial_h_values` has n=500 but sum not exactly n/2; project. Good.

Now final code will be around maybe 230 lines. Need ensure no line too long? okay.

Let's write final.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

_D = [0.0]

def _cb(x):
    return time.time() > _D[0]

def project(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return h
    target = 0.5 * n
    lo = -float(np.min(h))
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.max(h))
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    hp = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - hp.sum()
    if diff > 0.0:
        room = 1.0 - hp
        idx = np.flatnonzero(room > 1e-14)
        if idx.size:
            hp[idx] += diff * room[idx] / room[idx].sum()
    elif diff < 0.0:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(hp > 1e-14)
        if idx.size:
            hp[idx] += diff * hp[idx] / hp[idx].sum()
    return np.clip(hp, 0.0, 1.0)

def eval_c(h):
    return float(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").max())

def sob(x, n, beta, lam):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(c.max())
    z = beta * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(z)
    val = m + float(np.log(e.sum())) / beta
    err = float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    return val * (2.0 / n) + 0.5 * lam * err * err

def sgrad(x, n, beta, lam):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    m = float(c.max())
    z = beta * (c - m)
    z -= float(z.max())
    w = np.exp(z)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= sw
    s = n - 1
    first = np.convolve(w, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    g = first[s:2 * n - 1].copy()
    g -= np.convolve(w[::-1], x, mode="full")[s:2 * n - 1]
    g *= 2.0 / n
    if lam:
        g += lam * float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    return g

def lag_grad(h, idx, n):
    g = np.zeros(n, float)
    m = int(idx) - (n - 1)
    if m >= 0:
        l = n - m
        g[m:] += 1.0 - h[:l]
        g[:l] -= h[m:]
    else:
        r = -m
        l = n - r
        g[:l] += 1.0 - h[r:]
        g[r:] -= h[:l]
    return g

def lp_1(h, n, na):
    if cp is None:
        return None
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    mx = float(c.max())
    if c.size > na:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -na)[-na:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(c.size)
    extra = np.flatnonzero(c > mx - 2.0)
    if extra.size:
        idx = np.union1d(idx, extra)
    idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]]
    if idx.size > 70:
        idx = idx[:70]
    t = cp.Variable()
    H = cp.Variable((n, 1))
    cons = [H >= 0.0, H <= 1.0, cp.sum(H) == 0.5 * n]
    hf = h.reshape(n, 1)
    for ii in idx:
        g = lag_grad(h, int(ii), n).reshape(n, 1)
        cons.append(t >= float(c[ii]) + cp.sum(g * (H - hf)))
    prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(t), cons)
    status = None
    for sol in ("PDLP", "ECOS", "SCS"):
        try:
            prob.solve(solver=sol, verbose=False)
            status = prob.status
        except Exception:
            status = "error"
        if status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
            break
    if status not in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate") or H.value is None:
        return None
    xn = project(np.asarray(H.value).reshape(n))
    basec = eval_c(h)
    best = h
    bestc = basec
    for a in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.05, 0.02):
        cand = project(h + a * (xn - h))
        cc = eval_c(cand)
        if cc < bestc:
            bestc = cc
            best = cand
    if bestc < basec - 1e-12:
        return best, bestc * (2.0 / n)
    return None

def lp_polish(h, n, dl):
    h = project(h)
    bc = eval_c(h) * (2.0 / n)
    stall = 0
    na = 30
    while time.time() < dl - 3 and stall < 3:
        r = lp_1(h, n, na)
        if r is None:
            stall += 1
        else:
            nh, nc = r
            if nc < bc - 1e-12:
                h = nh
                bc = nc
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
        na = min(80, na + 10)
    return h, bc

def lbfgs(h, n, dl):
    best = project(h)
    bc = eval_c(best) * (2.0 / n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    _D[0] = dl - 2
    for beta in (5.0, 20.0, 80.0):
        if time.time() > dl - 3:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(sob, best, args=(n, beta, 100.0), jac=sgrad,
                           method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, callback=_cb,
                           options={"maxiter": 150, "ftol": 1e-16, "gtol": 1e-10})
            hn = project(res.x)
            cn = eval_c(hn) * (2.0 / n)
            if cn < bc:
                best = hn
                bc = cn
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, bc

def lowpass(h, cut):
    y = np.fft.rfft(h)
    yc = y.copy()
    if cut < len(yc):
        yc[cut + 1:] = 0.0
    hf = np.fft.irfft(yc, n=len(h))
    return project(hf)

def fourier_start(n, rng, nt, dl):
    x = 2.0 * np.arange(n) / n
    rows = []
    for m in range(1, 6):
        rows.append(np.cos(m * np.pi * x))
        rows.append(np.sin(m * np.pi * x))
    B = np.asarray(rows)
    best = None
    bc = 1e9
    for _ in range(nt):
        if time.time() > dl:
            break
        coeff = rng.normal(size=B.shape[0])
        nr = float(np.linalg.norm(coeff))
        if nr < 1e-12:
            continue
        g = (coeff / nr) @ B
        amax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if amax > 1.0:
            g /= amax
        h = project(0.5 + 0.5 * g)
        c = eval_c(h) * (2.0 / n)
        if c < bc:
            bc = c
            best = h
    return best, bc

def cands_d(lo, hi):
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    v = []
    mags = (1e-6, 1e-5, 3e-5, 1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 0.05, 0.1, 0.25)
    for m in mags:
        if -m > lo and -m < hi:
            v.append(-m)
        if m > lo and m < hi:
            v.append(m)
    span = hi - lo
    for f in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9):
        d = lo + span * f
        if d > lo + 1e-14 and d < hi - 1e-14:
            v.append(d)
    a = np.asarray(v, float)
    a = a[(a > lo + 1e-14) & (a < hi - 1e-14)]
    if a.size:
        a = np.unique(a)
    return a

def pair_step(h, c, p, q, n, cmx):
    hp = h[p]
    hq = h[q]
    lo = max(-hp, hq - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - hp, hq)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        return None
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = np.zeros(size)
    Q = np.zeros(size)
    mask = np.zeros(size, dtype=bool)
    rh = h[::-1]
    L[p:p + n] += 1.0 - rh
    L[n - 1 - p:2 * n - 1 - p] -= h
    mask[p:p + n] = True
    mask[n - 1 - p:2 * n - 1 - p] = True
    L[q:q + n] -= 1.0 - rh
    L[n - 1 - q:2 * n - 1 - q] += h
    mask[q:q + n] = True
    mask[n - 1 - q:2 * n - 1 - q] = True
    Q[n - 1] -= 2.0
    Q[n - 1 + p - q] += 1.0
    Q[n - 1 + q - p] += 1.0
    if mask.all():
        unaff = -np.inf
    else:
        unaff = float(c[~mask].max())
    base = c[mask]
    la = L[mask]
    qa = Q[mask]
    arr = cands_d(lo, hi)
    bd = 0.0
    bv = float(cmx)
    for d in arr:
        mm = float(np.max(base + la * d + qa * d * d))
        if unaff > mm:
            mm = unaff
        if mm < bv:
            bv = mm
            bd = d
    if bd != 0.0 and bv < cmx - 1e-12:
        h[p] += bd
        h[q] -= bd
        c[mask] = base + la * bd + qa * bd * bd
        return float(bv)
    return None

def pair_cd(h, n, dl, rng):
    h = project(np.asarray(h, float)).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    cmx = float(c.max())
    best = project(h)
    bestc = eval_c(best)
    target = 0.5 * n
    slack = min(5, max(2, n // 100))
    passes = 0
    while time.time() < dl - 2:
        passes += 1
        imp = 0
        order = np.argsort(np.minimum(h, 1.0 - h))[::-1]
        for s in order[:slack]:
            s = int(s)
            if h[s] <= 1e-12 or h[s] >= 1.0 - 1e-12:
                continue
            for p in range(n):
                if p == s:
                    continue
                if time.time() > dl - 2:
                    break
                r = pair_step(h, c, p, s, n, cmx)
                if r is not None:
                    cmx = r
                    imp += 1
            if time.time() > dl - 2:
                break
        if imp == 0:
            no = 0
            while time.time() < dl - 2 and no < 3000:
                p = int(rng.randint(n))
                q = int(rng.randint(n))
                if p == q:
                    no += 1
                    continue
                r = pair_step(h, c, p, q, n, cmx)
                if r is not None:
                    cmx = r
                    no = 0
                    imp = 1
                else:
                    no += 1
            if imp == 0:
                break
        if passes % 5 == 0 or abs(h.sum() - target) > 1e-9:
            hp = project(h)
            pc = eval_c(hp)
            if pc <= cmx + 1e-9:
                h = hp
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                cmx = float(c.max())
                if pc < bestc:
                    bestc = pc
                    best = h.copy()
            else:
                h = best.copy()
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
                cmx = float(c.max())
                bestc = cmx
    hp = project(h)
    pc = eval_c(hp)
    if pc < bestc:
        bestc = pc
        best = hp
    bp = project(best)
    bpc = eval_c(bp)
    if bpc < bestc:
        bestc = bpc
        best = bp
    return best, bestc * (2.0 / n)

def optimize_candidate(h, n, dl, rng):
    h = project(h)
    bc = eval_c(h) * (2.0 / n)
    lp1 = min(dl - 180, time.time() + 120)
    if time.time() < lp1 - 5:
        h1, bc1 = lp_polish(h, n, lp1)
        if bc1 < bc:
            h = project(h1)
            bc = bc1
    lbdl = dl - 160
    if time.time() < lbdl - 5:
        h1, c1 = lbfgs(h, n, lbdl)
        if c1 < bc:
            h = project(h1)
            bc = c1
    lp2 = min(dl - 60, time.time() + 80)
    if time.time() < lp2 - 5:
        h1, bc1 = lp_polish(h, n, lp2)
        if bc1 < bc:
            h = project(h1)
            bc = bc1
    if time.time() < dl - 3:
        h1, c1 = pair_cd(h, n, dl - 3, rng)
        if c1 < bc:
            h = project(h1)
            bc = c1
    h = project(h)
    bc = eval_c(h) * (2.0 / n)
    return h, bc

def cand_key(item):
    return item[1]

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    overall = t0 + budget_s - 6
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, float).copy()
        n = int(len(parent))
        parent = project(parent)
        pc = eval_c(parent) * (2.0 / n)
    except Exception:
        n = 400
        parent, pc = fourier_start(n, rng, 80, t0 + 20)
        if parent is None:
            parent = project(0.5 + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(n) / n))
            pc = eval_c(parent) * (2.0 / n)

    best_h = parent
    best_c = pc
    alts = []

    if n >= 80 and time.time() < overall - 180:
        for cut in (3, 8):
            hl = lowpass(parent, cut)
            cl = eval_c(hl) * (2.0 / n)
            if cl < pc + 0.002:
                alts.append((hl, cl))

    if n > 320 and time.time() < overall - 220:
        ns = 320
        x = 2.0 * np.arange(n) / n
        xs = 2.0 * np.arange(ns) / ns
        hd = project(np.interp(xs, x, parent))
        cd = eval_c(hd) * (2.0 / ns)
        if cd < pc + 0.002:
            alts.append((hd, cd))

    if time.time() < overall - 260:
        hf, cf = fourier_start(n, rng, 140, min(overall - 220, time.time() + 80))
        if hf is not None and cf < pc + 0.002:
            alts.append((hf, cf))

    if n > 320 and time.time() < overall - 300:
        hf2, cf2 = fourier_start(300, rng, 90, min(overall - 260, time.time() + 60))
        if hf2 is not None and cf2 < pc + 0.002:
            alts.append((hf2, cf2))

    if alts:
        alts.sort(key=cand_key)

    rem = overall - time.time()
    if rem > 150:
        dl_parent = time.time() + max(120.0, min(rem - 30.0, 0.62 * rem))
        hp, cp = optimize_candidate(best_h, n, dl_parent, rng)
        if cp < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = hp, cp

    for i, (ha, ca) in enumerate(alts):
        rem = overall - time.time()
        if rem < 100:
            break
        if ca > best_c + 0.003:
            continue
        if ca < best_c:
            share = 0.7
        elif i == 0:
            share = 0.35
        elif i == 1:
            share = 0.25
        else:
            share = 0.18
        dl_alt = time.time() + max(50.0, share * rem - 10.0)
        if dl_alt > overall - 20:
            dl_alt = overall - 20
        if dl_alt - time.time() < 35:
            break
        ho, co = optimize_candidate(ha, len(ha), dl_alt, rng)
        if co < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = ho, co

    best_h = project(best_h)
    diff = 0.5 * len(best_h) - float(best_h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        idx = int(np.argmax(np.minimum(best_h, 1.0 - best_h)))
        best_h = best_h.copy()
        best_h[idx] += diff
        best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    best_c = eval_c(best_h) * (2.0 / len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, int(len(best_h))
```
